**Amazon Review Summary Generator**
=====================================

This script takes in a list of Amazon product reviews and generates a summary of the review.

**Code**
```python
import nltk
from nltk.sentiment import SentimentIntensityAnalyzer
from nltk.corpus import stopwords
from nltk.stem import WordNetLemmatizer
from collections import Counter

# Initialize NLTK
-----
-----
2582
**Summary:** The reviewer is extremely dissatisfied with the product, calling it "useless crap" and stating that the quality is cheap.
-----
-----
1768
**Summary of Review:**

* The product arrived with a defective zipper pull.
* The product is lightweight but of poor quality.
* The zippers are also flimsy.
* The reviewer is returning the product.
-----
-----
2240
**Summary of Review:**
